{"etymology_number":"2","forms":[{"form":"scales","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["Part of an overlapping arrangement of many small, flat and hard pieces of keratin covering the skin of an animal, particularly a fish or reptile."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A small piece of pigmented chitin, many of which coat the wings of a butterfly or moth to give them their color."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A flake of skin of an animal afflicted with dermatitis."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Part of an overlapping arrangement of many small, flat and hard protective layers forming a pinecone that flare when mature to release pine nut seeds."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["The flaky material sloughed off heated metal."],"tags":["uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Scale mail (as opposed to chain mail)."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Limescale."],"tags":["uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A scale insect."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["The thin metallic side plate of the handle of a pocketknife."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["An infestation of scale insects on a plant; commonly thought of as, or mistaken for, a disease."],"tags":["US","uncountable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/skeɪl/","tags":["Canada","General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"[skeɪ̯(ə)ɫ]","tags":["Canada","General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/skæɪl/","tags":["Australia","New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"[skæɪ̯(ə)ɫ]","tags":["Australia","New-Zealand"]}],"upstream_line":167185,"upstream_offset":781546271,"upstream_record_sha256":"2dba9918c2e967368b5b3d1c442f53e1b05b1d5823017ae79cf2abeb750b6c88","word":"scale"}
